{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"blushes","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"glosses":["An act of blushing; a pink or red glow on the face caused by embarrassment, shame, shyness, love, etc."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A glow; a flush of colour, especially pink or red."],"synonyms":[{"word":"color"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A feeling or appearance of optimism."],"tags":["countable","figuratively","uncountable"]},{"glosses":["A cosmetic, frequently a powder, used to redden the cheeks and lips."],"synonyms":[{"word":"blusher"},{"word":"rouge"}],"tags":["countable","uncountable"],"topics":["cosmetics","lifestyle"]},{"glosses":["A color between pink and cream."],"tags":["countable","uncountable"]},{"alt_of":[{"word":"blush wine"}],"glosses":["Ellipsis of blush wine."],"tags":["US","abbreviation","alt-of","countable","ellipsis","uncountable"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈblʌʃ/"},{"ipa":"[ˈblʌʃ]"}],"upstream_line":226031,"upstream_offset":1113246057,"upstream_record_sha256":"8472c2b2c18f331757fcdee7fd598f460272cfc553feeef62a0d1ad6c9adfae0","word":"blush"}
{"etymology_number":"1","forms":[{"form":"blushes","tags":["present","singular","third-person"]},{"form":"blushing","tags":["participle","present"]},{"form":"blushed","tags":["participle","past"]},{"form":"blushed","tags":["past"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"verb","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"The love scene made him blush to the roots of his hair / to the tips of his ears.","type":"example"},{"text":"He wasn't used to this much attention, so he blushed as he saw dozens of pairs of eyes watching him.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To become red or pink in the face (and sometimes experience an associated feeling of warmth), especially due to shyness, love, shame, excitement, or embarrassment."],"synonyms":[{"word":"color"},{"word":"flush"},{"word":"crimson"}],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To be shy, ashamed, or embarrassed (to do something)."],"tags":["figuratively","intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To become red or pink."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To suffuse with a blush; to redden; to pinken; to make rosy."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"When he saw it, he blushed a beet red.","type":"example"},{"text":"I wasn't surprised, but it was embarrassing enough that I blushed a little pink.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To change skin color in the face (to a particular shade)."],"tags":["copulative"]},{"examples":[{"text":"Looking at me with a knowing glare, she blushed her discomfort with the situation.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To express or make known by blushing."],"tags":["transitive"]},{"examples":[{"text":"The garden was full of blossoms that blushed in myriad shades to form a beautiful carpet of color.","type":"example"}],"glosses":["To have a warm and delicate colour, like some roses and other flowers."],"tags":["intransitive"]},{"glosses":["To glance with the eye, cast a glance."],"tags":["intransitive","obsolete"]},{"glosses":["Of dope or varnish: to develop an undesirable white precipitate on the surface, due to being applied in humid conditions."],"tags":["intransitive"],"topics":["aeronautics","aerospace","aviation","business","engineering","natural-sciences","physical-sciences"]}],"sounds":[{"ipa":"/ˈblʌʃ/"},{"ipa":"[ˈblʌʃ]"}],"synonyms":[{"word":"flush"},{"word":"pinken"},{"word":"redden"}],"upstream_line":226032,"upstream_offset":1113274750,"upstream_record_sha256":"297867c7f4e677703e396cd3a90717b45762500c73bcb8d6317ad5659b1f0249","word":"blush"}
